# ChatHandler — Class Overview

<!-- documentation: model-written from source via the local LLM; review before trusting -->

# ChatHandler

`ChatHandler` is the central command dispatcher for the WoWVMaNGOS server, providing the interface through which Game Masters (GMs), administrators, and the server console interact with the live world. It parses text input from in-game chat or the remote console, resolves command permissions via RBAC and security levels, and delegates execution to specialized handler methods that manipulate accounts, characters, creatures, game objects, and server subsystems.

As a massive class split across numerous translation units, `ChatHandler` acts as the primary "control plane" for server administration. It does not contain core gameplay logic but rather the tools to inspect, debug, and override that logic at runtime.

## How the class is split

The class is divided into partials based on the domain of the commands they implement. Each partial contributes a set of `Handle*Command` methods and, in some cases, supporting infrastructure.

*   **`ChatHandler.Chat`**: The core engine. Implements command registration, parsing, argument extraction (including shift-links), security checks, and output formatting. It loads command definitions from the `command` table and RBAC permissions from `rbac_*` tables.
*   **`ChatHandler.AccountCommands`**: Manages user accounts and moderation. Handles account creation/deletion, password resets, security level changes, bans (account/IP/character), mutes, kicks, and anti-cheat/spam integration.
*   **`ChatHandler.CharacterCommands`**: Focuses on player character manipulation. Includes cheat toggles (god mode, fly, no cooldown), stat/level/reputation/honor modification, spell/skill learning, item management, and character lifecycle operations (restore/delete).
*   **`ChatHandler.CreatureCommands`**: Manages NPCs and creatures. Provides inspection of spawn data, modification of creature properties (entry, display ID, faction, movement), and extensive waypoint/escort editing tools.
*   **`ChatHandler.ObjectCommands`**: Manages Game Objects (doors, chests, props). Handles spawning, despawning, moving, rotating, and state manipulation of static world objects.
*   **`ChatHandler.TeleportCommands`**: Handles movement and teleportation. Implements `.go`, `.tele`, and related commands for moving players/groups to coordinates, creatures, objects, or predefined teleports.
*   **`ChatHandler.UnitCommands`**: Inspects and manipulates `Unit` objects (players and creatures). Covers stat modification, aura application/removal, damage dealing, fear/knockback, and movement generator debugging.
*   **`ChatHandler.LookupCommands`**: Provides search and inspection utilities. Allows GMs to look up entities (spells, items, quests, factions, accounts) by name or ID and retrieve detailed information.
*   **`ChatHandler.MiscCommands`**: Covers miscellaneous administrative tasks. Includes GM visibility/status, guild management, instance/dungeon control, mail sending, battleground administration, and help/navigation.
*   **`ChatHandler.ServerCommands`**: Manages server-wide operations. Handles reloading database tables, controlling server shutdown/restart, managing game events, adjusting weather, and configuring anti-spam filters.
*   **`ChatHandler.DebugCommands`**: Provides low-level debugging tools. Includes packet injection, field inspection/modification, MMAP/VMAP diagnostics, and stress testing utilities.
*   **`ChatHandler.HardcodedEvents`**: Manages specific persistent world events (Elemental Invasion, Darkmoon Faire, War Effort, etc.). Contains event controllers and admin commands to manipulate their state.
*   **`ChatHandler.PlayerBotMgr`**: Integrates the SuperUiBots system. Provides commands to spawn, control, and debug AI bots (party bots, battle bots, random bots) and manages their lifecycle via the `PlayerBotMgr` singleton.
*   **`ChatHandler.AuctionHouseBotMgr`**: Manages the auction house bot. Loads item definitions from the database and provides commands to update or reload the bot's configuration.
*   **`ChatHandler.ChatCommands`**: Handles specific subsystem commands, currently limited to monitoring and configuring the `MovementBroadcaster` (packet broadcast) threads.
*   **`ChatHandler.TicketCommands`**: Manages the GM Ticket system. Provides commands to view, assign, comment on, escalate, and close support tickets.

## How the partials collaborate

The collaboration pattern is centralized around the `ChatHandler.Chat` partial, which acts as the dispatcher.

1.  **Dispatch Flow**: When a command is issued, `ChatHandler.Chat` parses the input, checks permissions (using `GetAccessLevel` and RBAC data), and invokes the specific `Handle*Command` method located in the relevant partial (e.g., `ChatHandler.TeleportCommands/HandleGoCommand`).
2.  **Shared Infrastructure**: All partials rely on `ChatHandler.Chat` for:
    *   **Argument Extraction**: Methods like `ExtractUInt32`, `ExtractPlayerTarget`, and `ExtractSpellIdFromLink` are defined in `ChatHandler.Chat` and called by handlers in other partials.
    *   **Output**: Methods like `PSendSysMessage` and `SendSysMessage` are defined in `ChatHandler.Chat` to ensure consistent feedback to the issuer.
    *   **Security**: Methods like `HasLowerSecurity` enforce hierarchy rules across all command types.
3.  **Cross-Partial Dependencies**:
    *   `ChatHandler.UnitCommands` and `ChatHandler.CreatureCommands` share logic for AI info display (`HandleUnitAIInfoCommand` calls `HandleNpcAIInfoCommand`).
    *   `ChatHandler.PlayerBotMgr` integrates with `ChatHandler.ObjectCommands` (via `getSelectedGameObject`) for party bot interactions with game objects.
    *   `ChatHandler.HardcodedEvents` exposes commands that manipulate global event states, which are monitored by `ChatHandler.ServerCommands` (event list/info).
4.  **External Boundaries**: The class interacts extensively with:
    *   **Database**: Direct queries via `Database/PQuery`, `Database/PExecute` for account, character, creature, and game object data.
    *   **Singletons**: `World`, `ObjectMgr`, `AccountMgr`, `TicketMgr`, `PlayerBotMgr`, `AuctionHouseBotMgr`, `GameEventMgr`.
    *   **Entities**: `Player`, `Creature`, `GameObject`, `Unit`, `WorldSession`.

## Data model

`ChatHandler` interacts with numerous database tables to read and modify server state. Key tables include:

*   **`command`**: Stores command definitions, security levels, and help text. Loaded by `ChatHandler.Chat`.
*   **`rbac_permissions`, `rbac_account_permissions`, `rbac_command_permissions`**: Store Role-Based Access Control data. Loaded by `ChatHandler.Chat`.
*   **`account`**: Used for account management (`ChatHandler.AccountCommands`), lookups (`ChatHandler.LookupCommands`), and bot account generation (`ChatHandler.PlayerBotMgr`).
*   **`characters`**: Used for character management, deletion, and restoration (`ChatHandler.CharacterCommands`).
*   **`creature`, `creature_addon`**: Used for creature inspection and modification (`ChatHandler.CreatureCommands`).
*   **`gameobject`**: Used for game object inspection and modification (`ChatHandler.ObjectCommands`).
*   **`playerbot`**: Stores persistent bot definitions. Loaded by `ChatHandler.PlayerBotMgr`.
*   **`auctionhousebot`**: Stores auction bot item definitions. Loaded by `ChatHandler.AuctionHouseBotMgr`.
*   **`script_waypoint`, `script_escort_data`**: Used for waypoint and escort editing (`ChatHandler.CreatureCommands`).
*   **`antispam_blacklist`, `antispam_replacement`**: Used for anti-spam configuration (`ChatHandler.ServerCommands`).
*   **Various `character_*` tables**: `character_spell`, `character_skills`, `character_inventory`, etc., are accessed for character state modification.

## Where to go deeper

*   **`ChatHandler.Chat`**: For understanding command parsing, permission checks, and the core dispatch mechanism.
*   **`ChatHandler.AccountCommands`**: For account management, banning, and moderation tools.
*   **`ChatHandler.CharacterCommands`**: For player cheat toggles, stat modification, and character lifecycle management.
*   **`ChatHandler.CreatureCommands`**: For NPC manipulation, spawn editing, and waypoint/escort creation.
*   **`ChatHandler.ObjectCommands`**: For Game Object manipulation and debugging.
*   **`ChatHandler.TeleportCommands`**: For teleportation logic and coordinate validation.
*   **`ChatHandler.UnitCommands`**: For unit stat modification, aura manipulation, and combat debugging.
*   **`ChatHandler.LookupCommands`**: For entity search and inspection utilities.
*   **`ChatHandler.MiscCommands`**: For guild, instance, mail, and battleground administration.
*   **`ChatHandler.ServerCommands`**: For server reloading, event management, and shutdown/restart controls.
*   **`ChatHandler.DebugCommands`**: For low-level packet injection, field inspection, and MMAP/VMAP diagnostics.
*   **`ChatHandler.HardcodedEvents`**: For managing specific persistent world events.
*   **`ChatHandler.PlayerBotMgr`**: For integrating and controlling the SuperUiBots AI fleet.
*   **`ChatHandler.AuctionHouseBotMgr`**: For configuring the auction house bot.
*   **`ChatHandler.ChatCommands`**: For monitoring the movement broadcaster subsystem.
*   **`ChatHandler.TicketCommands`**: For managing the GM Ticket support system.

## Schema — verified columns (dumped from the live database)

- `account`: id int(11) unsigned PK, username varchar(32), gmlevel tinyint(3) unsigned, sessionkey longtext?, v longtext?, s longtext?, token_key varchar(100), email text?, joindate timestamp, last_ip varchar(30), failed_logins int(11) unsigned, locked tinyint(3) unsigned, lock_country varchar(2), last_login timestamp, online tinyint(4), expansion tinyint(3) unsigned, mutetime bigint(40), locale tinyint(3) unsigned, os varchar(4), platform varchar(4), current_realm tinyint(3) unsigned, flags int(10) unsigned, security varchar(255)?, email_verif tinyint(1), geolock_pin int(11)?
- `account_banned`: banid bigint(20), id bigint(20) PK, bandate bigint(40) PK, unbandate bigint(40), bannedby varchar(50), banreason varchar(255), active tinyint(4), realm tinyint(4), gmlevel tinyint(4) unsigned
- `account_data`: account int(11) unsigned PK, type int(11) unsigned PK, time bigint(11) unsigned, data longblob
- `auction`: id int(11) unsigned PK, house_id int(11) unsigned, item_guid int(11) unsigned, item_id int(11) unsigned, seller_guid int(11) unsigned, buyout_price int(11), expire_time bigint(40), buyer_guid int(11) unsigned, last_bid int(11), start_bid int(11), deposit int(11)
- `auctionhousebot`: item int(11) unsigned, stack tinyint(3) unsigned, bid int(11) unsigned, buyout int(11) unsigned
- `character_account_data`: guid int(11) unsigned PK, type int(11) unsigned PK, time bigint(11) unsigned, data longblob
- `character_gifts`: guid int(20) unsigned, item_guid int(11) unsigned PK, item_id int(20) unsigned, flags int(20) unsigned
- `character_inventory`: guid int(11) unsigned, bag int(11) unsigned, slot tinyint(3) unsigned, item_guid int(11) unsigned PK, item_id int(11) unsigned
- `character_pet`: id int(11) unsigned PK, entry int(11) unsigned, owner_guid int(11) unsigned, display_id int(11) unsigned?, created_by_spell int(11) unsigned, pet_type tinyint(3) unsigned, level int(11) unsigned, xp int(11) unsigned, react_state tinyint(1) unsigned, loyalty_points int(11), loyalty int(11) unsigned, training_points int(11), name varchar(100)?, renamed tinyint(1) unsigned, slot int(11) unsigned, current_health int(11) unsigned, current_mana int(11) unsigned, current_happiness int(11) unsigned, save_time bigint(20) unsigned, reset_talents_cost int(11) unsigned, reset_talents_time bigint(20) unsigned, action_bar_data longtext?, teach_spell_data longtext?
- `character_queststatus`: guid int(11) unsigned PK, quest int(11) unsigned PK, status int(11) unsigned, rewarded tinyint(1) unsigned, explored tinyint(1) unsigned, timer bigint(20) unsigned, mob_count1 int(11) unsigned, mob_count2 int(11) unsigned, mob_count3 int(11) unsigned, mob_count4 int(11) unsigned, item_count1 int(11) unsigned, item_count2 int(11) unsigned, item_count3 int(11) unsigned, item_count4 int(11) unsigned, reward_choice int(11) unsigned
- `character_skills`: guid int(11) unsigned PK, skill mediumint(9) unsigned PK, value mediumint(9) unsigned, max mediumint(9) unsigned
- `character_spell`: guid int(11) unsigned PK, spell int(11) unsigned PK, active tinyint(3) unsigned, disabled tinyint(3) unsigned
- `characters`: guid int(11) unsigned PK, account int(11) unsigned, name varchar(12), race tinyint(3) unsigned, class tinyint(3) unsigned, gender tinyint(3) unsigned, skin tinyint(3) unsigned, face tinyint(3) unsigned, hair_style tinyint(3) unsigned, hair_color tinyint(3) unsigned, facial_hair tinyint(3) unsigned, level tinyint(3) unsigned, xp int(10) unsigned, money int(10) unsigned, character_flags int(10) unsigned, zone int(11) unsigned, map int(11) unsigned, instance int(11) unsigned, position_x float, position_y float, position_z float, orientation float, transport_guid bigint(20) unsigned, transport_x float, transport_y float, transport_z float, transport_o float, known_taxi_mask longtext?, current_taxi_path text?, online tinyint(3) unsigned, played_time_total int(11) unsigned, played_time_level int(11) unsigned, create_time bigint(20) unsigned, logout_time bigint(20) unsigned, rest_bonus float, reset_talents_multiplier int(11) unsigned, reset_talents_time bigint(20) unsigned, death_expire_time bigint(20) unsigned, stable_slots tinyint(1) unsigned, bank_bag_slots tinyint(1) unsigned, extra_flags int(11) unsigned, honor_rank_points float, honor_highest_rank int(11) unsigned, honor_standing int(11) unsigned, honor_last_week_hk int(11) unsigned, honor_last_week_cp float, honor_stored_hk int(11), honor_stored_dk int(11), watched_faction int(11), drunk smallint(5) unsigned, health int(10) unsigned, power1 int(10) unsigned, power2 int(10) unsigned, power3 int(10) unsigned, power4 int(10) unsigned, power5 int(10) unsigned, explored_zones longtext?, equipment_cache longtext?, ammo_id int(10) unsigned, action_bars tinyint(3) unsigned, deleted_account int(11) unsigned?, deleted_name varchar(12)?, deleted_time bigint(20)?, world_phase_mask int(11)?
